**[01:09:26.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000491)

**[01:09:33.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000492)

**[01:09:39.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000494)  
I've been there.

**[01:09:39.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000495)

**[01:09:42.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000496)

**[01:09:51.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000498)

**[01:09:53.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000499)

**[01:09:56.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000500)

**[01:09:59.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000501)

**[01:10:03.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000502)

**[01:10:06.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000503)

Alam mo yan.

**[01:11:20.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000530)  
Lalo na yung Game Fixing.

Lalo na yung Game Fixing.

**[01:11:38.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000537)

**[01:11:47.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000540)

**[01:11:53.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000542)

**[01:27:43.040] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-000975)

**[01:30:27.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001059)  
At minsan,

**[01:45:39.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001510)

**[01:45:45.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001511)

**[01:45:50.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001512)

**[01:45:54.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001513)  
Sorry, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:46:03.480] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001516)

**[01:46:10.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001517)

**[01:46:13.460] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001518)

**[01:46:13.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001519)

**[01:46:15.880] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001520)  
Hindi po, worth of food.

**[01:46:18.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001521)  
Of food pala.

**[01:46:18.960] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001522)  
Ang underpour rito, Senator Joel, nasa batas po kasi ng Philippine Sports Commission

**[01:46:23.800] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001523)  
na kaya po kailangan makikumingi po ako ng tandaan.

**[01:46:27.180] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001524)  
Tulong sa inyo ma-amiendahan na yung Philippine Sports Commission Charter.

**[01:46:31.340] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001525)  
Kasi po, sa Charter ng Philippine Sports Commission ng 1990, nakasaad pa po ron

**[01:46:35.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001526)  
to serve only and to support only amateur athletes.

**[01:46:41.360] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001527)  
Kaya po, meron po may mga instances na nate-technical po kami

**[01:46:45.460] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001528)  
because we have national athletes who are professional players.

**[01:46:50.220] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001529)  
Kasi po, allowed na ang professional sa Olympics, allowed na po sa Asian Games.

**[01:46:57.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001530)  
Before, di ba, ang Sports Commission is under the, known as the DEX, di ba?

**[01:47:04.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001531)  
So, hiniwalay po ang Sports Commission sa education, naging deped, nahiwalay.

**[01:47:09.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001532)

**[01:47:18.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001533)

**[01:47:23.220] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001534)  
Kasi sabi ko nga, tuwing nananalo tayo,

**[01:47:26.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001535)

**[01:47:30.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001536)  
We are very proud na manalo ating mga atleta.

**[01:47:35.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001537)  
At importante rito, meron talagang nakatutok sa kanila na isang departamento,

**[01:47:39.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001538)

**[01:47:46.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001539)

**[01:47:49.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001540)

**[01:47:54.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001541)  
Maybe we can study.

**[01:47:56.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001542)  
It's here, no?

**[01:47:57.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001543)  
Through legislation.

**[01:47:59.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001544)  
At kayo ba sangayon ba kayo kung sakasakali na kailangan ba ng isang departamento

**[01:48:03.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001545)  
para mas matutukan nyo po ang ating mga atleta?

**[01:48:08.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001546)  
Panapapanahon na ba?

**[01:48:09.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001547)  
Kasi, for example, my fellow...

**[01:48:11.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001548)  
You should study that really in-depth study because my next question would be

**[01:48:17.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001549)

**[01:48:22.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001550)  
You have to convince us that...

**[01:48:26.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001551)  
There is a big difference as to your operations when you become a department

**[01:48:32.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001552)  
and what are expected of you.

**[01:48:35.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001553)  
And more importantly, I think, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:48:38.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001554)  
yung maganda na nabanggit ni Chairman Pato yung sa mga atletes,

**[01:48:43.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001555)  
basta ikaw ay nag-represent ng Philippine team.

**[01:48:46.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001556)  
Ikaw ay saklaw nitong atletes' benefits.

**[01:48:51.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001557)  
So, ibig sabihin,

**[01:48:53.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001558)  
yung...

**[01:48:54.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001559)  
yun nga, yung mga atletes natin na nag-represent sa Asian Games,

**[01:48:59.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001560)  
kasama po sila dyan.

**[01:49:01.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001561)  
Until when?

**[01:49:02.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001562)  
Gusto ko lang maintindihan, until when?

**[01:49:04.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001563)  
By the duration?

**[01:49:06.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001564)  
After the Asian Games?

**[01:49:08.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001565)  
For instance, tapos na po yun?

**[01:49:09.860] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001566)  
Opo.

**[01:49:10.620] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001567)  
Ano ito po, Senator Joel?

**[01:49:13.280] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001568)  
Nati-technical lang po kami because of our charter and our mandate.

**[01:49:17.260] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001569)  
On the definition of professional athletes.

**[01:49:19.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001570)

**[01:49:23.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001571)  
Yung professionals po.

**[01:49:24.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001572)  
But the professionals supposed to be, they're, I mean, more...

**[01:49:28.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001573)  
Diba? Mas marami na silang...

**[01:49:29.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001574)  
Pondo.

**[01:49:30.560] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001575)  
Pondo.

**[01:49:31.160] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001576)  
Opo.

**[01:49:31.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001577)  
But of course, it's not an excuse.

**[01:49:33.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001578)  
I don't want to sound like we don't care about them.

**[01:49:35.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001579)  
Of course, it's not an excuse.

**[01:49:37.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001580)  
But ang point ko lang is, at least, lahat sila, no?

**[01:49:41.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001581)  
Ngayon, tapos na yung Asian Games.

**[01:49:42.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001582)  
Wala na ba yun?

**[01:49:44.500] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001583)  
Tuloy-tuloy po yan para sa mga atleteng considered amateur.

**[01:49:48.600] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001584)  
Tuloy-tuloy po yan as far as uniforms.

**[01:49:51.640] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001585)  
Tuloy-tuloy po yan in terms of...

**[01:49:54.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001586)  
Yung training nila abroad.

**[01:49:55.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001587)  
Itay ko na to, chairman, no?

**[01:49:57.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001588)  
Yung opportunity because I didn't make it during the hearing.

**[01:50:01.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001589)  
Nag-usap na po kayo nung PNVF, yung pagbalik nyo nung Nagoya, diba?

**[01:50:06.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001590)  
Let's just talk about specifics dito sa volleyball, no?

**[01:50:09.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001591)  
Nagbigay na yung PSC ng transition report.

**[01:50:12.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001592)  
Pwede malaman ano yung exactong napagkasundoan nyo nitong PNVF

**[01:50:17.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001593)  
at ano yung maaasahan nating improvements ng ating mga atleta mula ngayon

**[01:50:21.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001594)  
in terms of allowance, training, and other athletes.

**[01:50:24.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001595)  
Kami po ni Sen. JV. We're very close to a lot of athletes.

**[01:50:28.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001596)  
Sen. JV po ang isa sa mga pioneers nitong V-League.

**[01:50:34.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001597)  
Is it V-League, Brad? Yung sa San Juan, no?

**[01:50:37.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001598)  
So nandun kami, magkasama kami matagal na and we have talked to a lot of players,

**[01:50:43.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001599)  
volleyball players, and we were surprised to hear a lot of complaints, a lot of...

**[01:50:49.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001600)  
And of course, it's nothing to do with the Philippine Sports Commission,

**[01:50:52.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001601)  
but you being the...

**[01:50:54.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001602)  
the PSC, I think you can do a lot of things in addressing these issues.

**[01:51:01.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001603)  
Madalas ho kasi, and I will say this with all humility,

**[01:51:04.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001604)  
na madalas ho kasi ayaw natin makialam dahil,

**[01:51:07.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001605)  
hindi, sila-sila na yan eh, problema nila na yan.

**[01:51:10.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001606)  
Pero kung wala ho talagang makikialam, chairman,

**[01:51:14.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001607)  
kawawa talaga yung mga atleta ho natin.

**[01:51:16.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001608)  
We have seen this, you know, I am not an expert,

**[01:51:19.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001609)  
especially when you talk about volleyball, I am not an expert.

**[01:51:22.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001610)  
Until now, probably, I don't know.

**[01:51:24.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001611)  
It's in volleyball, but yung mga naririnig ho namin,

**[01:51:27.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001612)  
and just a personal experience, for instance,

**[01:51:31.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001613)  
dun sa board nila, walang volleyball player,

**[01:51:33.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001614)  
walang talagang tumututok para hanapin or alamin kung ano talaga yung nangyayari sa mga atleta.

**[01:51:42.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001615)  
And yun, negosyo ba yun?

**[01:51:44.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001616)  
It's a corporation now.

**[01:51:46.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001617)  
Parang yun lang ho, it's a personal comment.

**[01:51:49.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001618)  
Again, I'm not an expert, but it pains me a lot as a former athlete,

**[01:51:54.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001619)  
atlet na, yun lang eh, yung maririnig mo na representing the Philippines,

**[01:51:58.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001620)  
wala kang pang transportation, wala kang maayos na pagkain,

**[01:52:03.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001621)  
and higit sa lahat, yung sinasabi ng mga players, first-hand information po,

**[01:52:07.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001622)  
yung mga sinasabi ng players na,

**[01:52:09.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001623)  
oy, yung sponsor daw, kailangan,

**[01:52:13.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001624)  
kailangan huwag kang magsusuot ng, ano tawag ho doon, yung leggings.

**[01:52:18.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001625)  
Dapat daw, walang leggings para kita legs.

**[01:52:20.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001626)  
Things like that, nakakainit ho ng ulo eh.

**[01:52:24.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001627)  
You know, I can go on and on.

**[01:52:26.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001628)  
In fact, if we will push with the investigation in the Blue Ribbon Committee,

**[01:52:33.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001629)  
pag nakuha ko lahat yung mga papeles na yan at mga testigo na yan,

**[01:52:37.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001630)  
talagang patawag ho natin yung mga officials, raw information.

**[01:52:42.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001631)  
That's why I don't want to divulge it right now, but it's really alarming.

**[01:52:45.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001632)  
It's really alarming.

**[01:52:46.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001633)  
And I'm only talking about volleyball here, I'm not talking about any other sports.

**[01:52:50.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001634)  
Just to put that on record.

**[01:52:51.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001635)  
So, Mr. Chairman, pwedeng malaman lang yung developments mo?

**[01:52:54.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001636)  
With this particular NSA, itong PNVF, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:52:59.260] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001637)  
Thank you, Senator Joel.

**[01:53:01.120] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001638)  
Alam nyo po yung PNVF, nagkaroon talaga sila ng major issues dyan sa leadership.

**[01:53:07.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001639)  
They, nakatatlong presidente na ho yan in 12 months.

**[01:53:11.580] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001640)  
Opo.

**[01:53:12.380] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001641)  
So, nakatatlong presidente sila in 12 months, palit sila ng palit ng signatory,

**[01:53:16.300] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001642)  
palit sila ng palit ng programa, hanggang na, tinamaan na ho sila, inahabol po.

**[01:53:21.760] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001643)  
But, we are...

**[01:53:24.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001644)  
We are trying to solve that.

**[01:53:25.140] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001645)  
As a matter of fact, tomorrow, I have a meeting with them.

**[01:53:27.820] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001646)  
With the new president, with the officers, we have a meeting with them.

**[01:53:30.860] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001647)  
Kasi kasama po sa tatalakayan namin yung mga unliquidated nila,

**[01:53:34.600] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001648)  
na ginasto sila, na hindi nila naliliquidated, naliliquidate.

**[01:53:39.760] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001649)  
Kasama po yan.

**[01:53:41.000] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001650)  
So, bukas po, kamiting namin siyang bago presidente, Frank Lau.

**[01:53:44.660] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001651)  
At nangako naman po siya na tutulungan natin sila.

**[01:53:47.260] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001652)  
Pero, rinireview rin po namin, Senator Joel, yung definition talaga ng professionals.

**[01:53:54.080] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001653)  
Ako,

**[01:53:54.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001654)  
I'm with you, sir.

**[01:53:55.980] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-001655)

**[02:30:00.000] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003044)  
Third floor na po kami kung di po ako nagkakamali. Hanggang seven floors lang naman po yan. Kailangan lang po namin tapusin kasi nagawa na.

**[02:30:07.700] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003045)  
Ito po yung Cebu Regional Training Center, Chairman JV. Ito po yung usapan natin last month kung paano.

**[02:30:17.280] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003046)  
Nagbigay po ang Cebu City ng 1,000 square meters para itaguyod yung Arnis Gymnastics and Danceport Facility.

**[02:30:28.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003047)  
Tutulong rin po si Senator Mig Zubiri para maitayo po ito. Sinabi niya, "Tutulungan ko kayong pondohan iyan." Ito yung track and field oval po sa PhilSports na hindi na na-renovate since the late 80s, na kailangan po talaga nating pagandahin para magamit sa international competitions. Ito po yung UP Mindanao Aquatic Center na nakabinbin na tinatapos na rin po natin.

**[02:30:57.240] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003048)  
Yan po.

**[02:30:58.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003049)  
Ito po. Ongoing po yan. Kailangan na rin po ng pondo para tapusin.

**[02:31:03.640] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003050)  
Ito po, Senator JV, Chairman, yung sinabi niyo sa akin na facility sa Cebu na nakakalungkot kasi in three years time ganyan na po siya.

**[02:31:13.900] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003051)  
Meron na po kaming initial budget na pinagawa ni Commissioner Ed Heiko.

**[02:31:19.860] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003052)  
Papunta po ako sa Cebu sa October 16 para personal na inspect.

**[02:31:24.700] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003053)  
Sabi ko nga po, sana matulungan mo kami rito.

**[02:31:28.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003054)  
So, Center, kasi kaya na po natin paspasan ito para matapos po.

**[02:31:33.160] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003055)  
Kapareho po ito nung ginawa namin sa Baguio na nagumastos po kami sa Baguio ng mga 40 million, Center JV.

**[02:31:40.580] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003056)  
Ito po yung sa Rizal na talagang kailangan na rin i-rehabilitate yung swimming pool stadium sa Rizal kasi po maganda na po yung Rizal.

**[02:31:50.980] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003057)  
Ito po yung sa Imus na sinabi po sa akin ng province of Cavite, handa silang magbigay ng 300 million for an aquatic center.

**[02:31:58.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003058)  
Kung meron lang po kaming counterpart na may bibigay rin.

**[02:32:03.040] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003059)  
Ito po yung gustong-gustong natin lahat, yung Philippine International Tennis Center, Alex Ayala Tennis Center sa New Clark City po ito.

**[02:32:11.240] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003060)  
Ito po ay isang 2 billion project na pwedeng gawin kagad, may lupa na po ron.

**[02:32:18.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003061)  
And this will really be the premier Philippine Tennis Center with around 16 courts.

**[02:32:25.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003062)  
16 courts po ito.

**[02:32:28.100] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003063)  
Sana magawa na natin kasi talagang sayang po yung pagtaguyod ng tennis sa Pilipinas.

**[02:32:35.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003064)  
And ayan po naman yung mga international events natin na hino-host.

**[02:32:40.700] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003065)  
Yan po yung isang tatamaan sa lalo na po yung hosting natin ng Southeast Asian Youth Games Plus and Asian Beach Games sa 2027 and 2028.

**[02:32:49.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003066)  
So basically, Chairman, yung mga tinatamaan po are the events that we want.

**[02:32:57.940] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003067)  
We want to host to really promote the sport.

**[02:33:01.360] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003068)  
And the regional training centers po, I think this will be our legacy projects.

**[02:33:06.500] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003069)

**[02:33:15.800] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003070)

**[02:33:20.620] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003071)

panahon ng...

**[02:33:34.660] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003074)

**[02:44:42.820] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003358)

**[02:45:00.000] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003367)

**[02:45:18.220] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003368)

**[02:46:12.540] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003372)  
28 po.

**[02:47:11.180] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003382)

**[02:47:17.560] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003383)  
And I'm behind.

**[02:47:18.220] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003384)

**[02:47:26.480] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003385)

**[02:47:31.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003386)

**[02:47:36.840] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003387)

**[02:47:44.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003388)

**[02:47:47.340] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003389)

**[02:47:48.060] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003390)

**[02:47:59.980] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003392)  
And the guidance of the Commission on Audit po.

**[02:48:04.560] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003393)  
So yan po ang example.

**[02:48:05.940] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003394)  
So bilang paggawa ka, Mr. Chairman, gusto ko lang po ipaliwanag nga sa inyo na ang aming NEP ngayon is nasa 1.349B.

**[02:48:18.060] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003395)

**[02:48:28.500] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003397)

**[02:48:36.160] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003398)

**[02:48:40.240] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003399)

**[02:48:45.200] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003400)

**[02:48:51.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003401)

**[02:49:03.560] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003402)

**[02:49:10.120] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003403)  
And if we can...

**[02:49:20.020] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003405)

**[02:49:28.480] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003406)

**[02:49:35.360] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003407)

**[02:49:41.920] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003408)

**[02:49:44.560] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003409)  
At...

**[02:50:23.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003420)  
Anyway, before we proceed with the gab, meron lang po tayong isang national athlete rin po na nasa Zoom lang po.

**[02:50:36.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003421)  
Meron siyang question o hinahing.

**[02:50:40.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003422)

**[02:50:44.920] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003423)  
Meron tayong isang Jiu-Jitsu medalist na rin po na lumapit sa ating opisina na magpapatulong po para ma-follow up sa PSC.

**[02:50:51.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003424)  
Incentive nila na napanalunan noong Jiu-Jitsu World Championship noong August 26.

**[02:50:58.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003425)  
Nandito po si Kimberly Ann Custodio.

**[02:51:01.800] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003426)  
Ah, yeah. Kimberly, si Chairman Patupo ito.

**[02:51:05.780] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003427)  
Yes, pinoproseso na namin yung incentives nyo.

**[02:51:09.240] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003428)  
But just to put on record that yung pag-submit ho ng record...

**[02:51:14.920] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003429)

**[02:51:20.080] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003430)  
So, in the next few weeks, hopefully in the next few days, i-release na po namin yan.

**[02:51:27.160] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003431)  
Pero yung event po na nanalo kayo, congratulations po. Ito ay huling August.

**[02:51:40.700] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003433)  
So, I assure you, pagdating sa incentives...

**[02:51:44.920] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003434)  
Talaga, kung hindi namin pinagdadamot yan, as a matter of fact, even the youth athletes get more than what they expect

**[02:51:52.040] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003435)  
and more than any amount they have expected in their young career,

**[02:52:00.240] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003436)  
hindi po ano yan.

**[02:52:02.500] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003437)  
Yan po ay may pondo, yan po ay laging nakalaan, lagi pong nakasubaybay ang pag-core dyan.

**[02:52:09.460] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003438)  
So, matatanggap mo na yan kasi pinoproseso na namin yan.

**[02:52:12.580] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003439)  
So, thank you for...

**[02:52:14.920] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003440)

**[02:52:17.480] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003441)  
But like what I always say, and let it be very clear to everyone,

**[02:52:22.480] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003442)  
kami po ay makakapagtrabaho nung mabilis kung yung counterpart NSA po namin ay mabilis din.

**[02:52:31.040] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003443)  
Dahil kung ang counterpart NSA po namin ay hindi mabilis, kami po ay matiteknikal pagbilisan namin yung isang hindi pa sinasubmit sa amin.

**[02:52:41.320] PSC Chairman John Patrick C. Gregorio** (asr-003444)  
Sana po maunaw.

**[02:55:23.530] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003451)  
Bong Go, I'll make a brief presentation to introduce the Games and Amusements Board to the body. The Games and Amusements Board is the agency in charge of regulating and supervising professional sports in the Philippines. Oftentimes, it is mistaken for its bigger cousin, which is called the Philippine Sports Commission.

**[02:55:44.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003452)  
The Games and Amusements Board currently supervises a total of 17 non-contact sports and 6 contact sports.

**[02:55:56.560] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003453)  
The Games and Amusements Board, in the course of supervising and regulating professional sports, collects regulatory fees.

**[02:56:05.820] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003454)  
In 2025, it collected a total of 30 million pesos.

**[02:56:10.560] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003455)  
Aside from regulating and supervising professional sports,

**[02:56:14.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003456)  
the Games and Amusements Board is also in charge of the betting aspect of horse racing and the licensing of international derbies.

**[02:56:24.680] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003457)  
It also undertakes law enforcement operations in coordination with the Philippine National Police and the National Bureau of Investigation.

**[02:56:33.760] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003458)  
In 2025, the GAMS operations led to the arrest of a total of 194 persons,

**[02:56:40.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003459)  
out of whom,

**[02:56:43.320] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003460)  
145 were charged,

**[02:56:44.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003461)  
This year, the GAMS operations led to the arrest of 14 persons,

**[02:56:51.840] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003462)  
out of whom,

**[02:56:52.740] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003463)  
6 were charged in court.

**[02:56:55.480] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003464)  
For 2026,

**[02:56:58.640] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003465)  
the GAMS priority programs include

**[02:57:01.740] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003466)  
the continuing education of its licensees,

**[02:57:06.820] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003467)  
the Games and Amusements Board for this purpose organized seminars.

**[02:57:10.900] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003468)  
With respect to boxing,

**[02:57:13.260] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003469)  
these seminars involved sports,

**[02:57:15.060] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003470)  
training,

**[02:57:16.280] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003471)  
and

**[02:57:17.280] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003472)  
nutrition and conditioning.

**[02:57:19.620] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003473)  
It has been observed that

**[02:57:21.540] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003474)  
our Filipino boxers

**[02:57:23.140] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003475)  
are already competent

**[02:57:25.480] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003476)  
in terms of their skill

**[02:57:27.500] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003477)  
and they can become world champions.

**[02:57:30.680] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003478)  
However,

**[02:57:31.680] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003479)  
it is

**[02:57:32.400] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003480)  
rough.

**[02:57:36.480] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003481)  
So,

**[02:57:37.020] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003482)  
to address this,

**[02:57:38.700] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003483)  
we have seminars on nutrition and conditioning.

**[02:57:42.160] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003484)  
The Games and Amusements Board

**[02:57:43.980] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003485)  
is also gonna be for the next season.

**[02:57:44.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003486)  
for an ISO accreditation.

**[02:57:47.960] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003487)  
With this move,

**[02:57:49.200] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003488)  
the Games and Amusements Board

**[02:57:50.520] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003489)  
seeks to

**[02:57:53.040] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003490)  
elevate

**[02:57:53.500] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003491)  
its procedures and processes

**[02:58:00.620] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003492)  
up to international standards.

**[02:58:04.100] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003493)  
And finally,

**[02:58:05.440] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003494)  
the Games and Amusements Board

**[02:58:06.620] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003495)  
is currently crafting its

**[02:58:08.800] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003496)  
rules of procedure. The aim is

**[02:58:10.900] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003497)  
to afford our stakeholders

**[02:58:13.000] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003498)  
a speedy and an

**[02:58:14.820] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003499)  
inexpensive determination of their

**[02:58:16.880] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003500)  
administrative cases.

**[02:58:19.600] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003501)  
The National

**[02:58:20.740] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003502)  
Expenditure Program proposes

**[02:58:22.980] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003503)  
a budget of P175

**[02:58:25.280] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003504)  
million for the

**[02:58:26.800] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003505)  
Games and Amusements Board.

**[02:58:29.520] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003506)  
We pray that after

**[02:58:30.740] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003507)  
due consideration, the proposed

**[02:58:33.080] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003508)  
budget be approved.

**[02:58:34.800] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003509)  
Thank you.

**[02:58:42.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003510)  
I would like to speak

**[02:58:44.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003511)  
to the

**[02:58:44.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003512)  
GAB, yung napag-usapan kanina, yung game fixing, baka ma-address ninyo ito. It's been a problem ever since. Baka hindi lang po sa basketball. Silipin ninyo po nang mabuti. Trabaho ninyo naman po iyon.

**[02:59:05.320] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003513)  
Yes, Senator Bong.

**[02:59:08.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003514)  
And some questions lang po. What measures does the GAB currently implement

**[02:59:11.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003515)  
to protect the rights

**[02:59:12.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003516)  
and welfare of professional

**[02:59:13.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003517)  
athletes?

**[02:59:14.800] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003518)  
At present, Senator Bong, we are

**[02:59:24.700] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003519)  
Assured that before they are penalized or before they get disciplined, they'll be afforded their full right to due process of law

**[02:59:38.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003520)  
About some medical insurance, yung gabho ba require professional sports promoters, leagues, and organizers to provide medical insurance or accident coverage to participating athletes?

**[02:59:57.560] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003521)  
Yes, Senator. For example,

**[03:00:00.000] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003522)  
For example, for motorsports, the Games and Amusements Board requires that before we license a player, he must first submit an insurance coverage.

**[03:00:11.580] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003523)  
But this requirement is meeting with a lot of difficulties because our insurance companies generally do not cover risks involving professional athletes, especially on sports that are considered risky, like motorsports and boxing.

**[03:00:33.520] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003524)  
Wala namang reklamo itong mga professional athletes natin.

**[03:00:37.900] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003525)  
Our professional athletes guaranteed timely payouts.

**[03:00:41.580] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003526)  
Payment of their salaries, yung kanilang fight purses, yung prices, and other contractual compensation.

**[03:00:51.220] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003527)  
So far, Senator, we haven't received this year any complaint for non-payment of salaries.

**[03:00:57.640] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003528)  
Now, last year, there were complaints involving basketball players, but those complaints were withdrawn and it appears to us that they were addressed by their teams.

**[03:01:10.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003529)  
A question.

**[03:01:11.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003530)  
Chairman Pato, itong sa volleyball, basketball, meron tayong PBA, sanctioned by the GAB.

**[03:01:22.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003531)  
Sa volleyball naman, we have this Philippine Volleyball League, sa Women's.

**[03:01:27.800] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003532)  
Premier Volleyball League.

**[03:01:28.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003533)  
Premier Volleyball League. Sa inyo po yun?

**[03:01:32.140] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003534)  
Yes, Senator Bong.

**[03:01:33.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003535)  
Sa inyo sila kumukuha ng lisensya?

**[03:01:35.880] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003536)  
Yes, Senator Bong.

**[03:01:36.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003537)  
Sanction nyo po sila during games, nandiyo dyan kayo, you attend, you witness.

**[03:01:41.580] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003538)  
Yes, Senator Bong.

**[03:01:43.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003539)  
How about this sa men's volleyball? Kasi wala silang professional.

**[03:01:49.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003540)  
So ano pwedeng ma-explain nyo po sa ating mga kababayan, including Chairman Pato, ano ho bang pinakaiba na ngayon sa women's volleyball with the men's since walang professional yung men's.

**[03:02:10.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003541)  
And then yung kanilang mga matatanggap sa inyo, sa PSC, na tulong since they are now professionals.

**[03:02:24.220] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003542)  
Yes, Senator Bong.

**[03:02:26.480] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003543)  
The PSC and the Games and Amusements Board have a joint resolution.

**[03:02:31.840] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003544)  
In that resolution, we demarcate the line between professionals as against non-professionals.

**[03:02:39.080] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003545)  
Now, the general definition of a professional is one who participates in a sporting event, and in that participation he's paid a sum of money, either a salary or as a prize. Now, if you're paid a sum of money for participating in a sporting event

**[03:03:01.920] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003580)  
as a professional.

**[03:03:04.880] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-003581)  
That's the definition of who constitutes a professional athlete.

**[03:03:09.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003582)  
So wala na sa PSC, wala na silang natatanggap sa na-allowances and benefits from PSC since they are now nasa Pro League na po sila, kahit na nag-represent sila sa country. Ano yung matatanggap pa rin nila sa inyo sa PSC since they are representing the country even though they are professionals?

**[03:03:36.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003583)  
Good afternoon, Chairman. Professional players, when they represent the country in international competition through the National Sports Association, are still able to receive what amateur athletes get, for example free airfare, accommodation, allowances, and training requirements, just like the volleyball players when they participated in the Southeast Asian Games.

**[03:04:06.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003584)  
In the Pro League, they requested, this is volleyball, sir, they requested for an allowance. The board approved. In fact, at the time, the volleyball is under POC. So for as long as the professional player, whatever sports they are, they will be endorsed by the National Sports Federation to represent the country in international competition.

**[03:04:29.700] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003585)  
They can receive allowances and other expenses that required for the team.

**[03:04:36.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003586)  
To participate in the international competition. Especially next year, if you, for the information of the chair, I think of all the sports now, only wrestling has no professional competition.

**[03:04:54.540] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003587)  
That is the only remaining sports that is amateur in nature. But the rest of the sports from athletics up to in PAC, even boobinam, they have professional sports already.

**[03:05:06.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003588)  
So right now, the only thing that we cannot fund is when they represent the club. But if they represent the country, the PSC can still fund them.

**[03:05:17.240] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003589)  
Wala na silang regular monthly allowances no more?

**[03:05:20.460] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003590)  
Wala silang monthly allowances, sir, because for example, the volleyball women, they were not endorsed to us by the Federation for allowances.

**[03:05:30.840] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003591)  
However, if they represent the country, they are endorsed for allowances at the same time.

**[03:05:36.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003592)  
We approve.

**[03:05:37.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003593)  
So yung nag-compete sila sa Asian Games, meron?

**[03:05:39.800] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003594)  
Meron, sir.

**[03:05:41.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003595)  
Anong duration lang po ang pano nila?

**[03:05:43.880] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003596)  
Depende sa kanilang, for example, they go into camp for two weeks, the NEC requested, it will be approved for as long as it related to the international competition.

**[03:05:55.680] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003597)  
For example, Asian Games.

**[03:05:57.520] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003598)  
I think one of the reason this volleyball issue came up because every time they go to international competition, they have at least one month or two.

**[03:06:06.580] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003599)  
Two weeks training camp.

**[03:06:08.740] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003600)  
And I think it was not endorsed to us by the Federation at that time.

**[03:06:13.300] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003601)  
And I think those are some of the issues that we're going to...

**[03:06:15.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003602)  
So hindi kaagad sila nakahingi ng tulong sa inyo?

**[03:06:18.620] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003603)  
Yes, sir, because the NEC is the one who knows where to, these players, where they're going to do their camp, where they're going to do their exposure abroad.

**[03:06:29.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003604)  
Yan ang dapat ninyong pag-usapan, isa sa mga dapat ninyong pag-usapan with the...

**[03:06:36.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003605)  
NSAs.

Andiyan na siya? May tanong ko siya.

**[03:06:46.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003607)  
Wala.

**[03:08:49.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003636)  
Sa PSC, sa budget

**[03:08:56.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003637)  
regarding financial assistance to athletes,

**[03:09:00.040] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003638)  
the PSC has a budget for financial assistance to athletes,

**[03:09:03.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003639)  
including community sports development, sports الخ,

This program provides assistance

to local government units,

**[03:09:12.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003643)  
schools, community organizations,

**[03:09:15.920] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003645)  
for the conduct of grassroots sports activities.

**[03:09:19.020] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003646)  
Malaking bagay po ito dun natin

**[03:09:20.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003647)

**[03:09:22.220] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003648)

**[03:09:25.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003649)

Since sabi ko nga,

**[03:09:32.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003651)  
wala kayong regional offices,

**[03:09:33.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003652)  
since kulang kayo sa manpower,

**[03:09:36.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003653)  
ito po yung isang paraan

**[03:09:38.200] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003654)  
to promote sports sa grassroots level.

**[03:09:41.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003655)  
Malaking bagay po ito sa kanila

**[03:09:44.080] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003656)  
at bawat piso na tulong po

**[03:09:47.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003657)

**[03:09:49.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003658)  
ay malaking bagay po ito

**[03:09:50.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003659)  
to promote sports.

**[03:09:55.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003660)  
However, we also want to raise concerns

**[03:09:57.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003661)

and release of financial assistance.

May mga LGUs po,

**[03:10:03.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003664)  
at sports organizations po

**[03:10:05.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003665)  
na umahasa sa tulong ng PSC

**[03:10:07.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003666)  
para may sagawa ang kanilang sports programs

**[03:10:09.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003667)  
at competitions.

**[03:10:10.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003668)

**[03:10:13.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003669)  
napektuhan din po ang kanilang paghahanda,

**[03:10:15.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003670)

at higit sa lahat ang mga atletang

**[03:10:18.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003672)  
dapat makinabang sa mga programang ito.

**[03:10:21.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003673)  
May we ask the PSC

**[03:10:22.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003674)  
what is the usual processing period

**[03:10:24.760] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003675)  
from the submission of a request

We have received

**[03:10:33.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003680)

and other administrative concerns.

**[03:10:59.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003690)  
Ano bang naging problema?

**[03:11:02.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003692)

**[03:11:09.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003694)  
kung maaari nga po

**[03:11:10.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003695)  
mapabilis po yung

**[03:11:12.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003696)  
pagbibigay ng tulong sa kanila.

**[03:11:14.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003697)

**[03:11:15.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003698)  
ay pumupunta pa ng Metro Manila

**[03:11:17.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003699)  
to get the assistance po.

**[03:11:20.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003700)  
Tapos,

**[03:11:21.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003701)

o yung mga pangailangan nila doon

**[03:11:25.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003703)  
sa kanilang mga events.

**[03:11:27.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003704)  
Maaari nyo bang explain po

**[03:11:31.060] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003706)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:11:32.400] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003707)  
I think,

**[03:11:32.900] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003708)  
for the LGUs

**[03:11:35.040] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003709)  
who are requesting

**[03:11:36.780] PSC OIC Executive Director Atty. Guillermo B. Iroy Jr.** (asr-003710)  
for financial assistance

through the Community Sports Development,

on playing

kasi po itong

sports

**[03:29:57.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004552)  
kailangan po ito

**[03:29:58.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004553)  
ng focus talaga

**[03:29:59.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004554)  
lahat

**[03:30:00.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004555)  
Lahat ng sports, kita mo sa golf, kailangan nakatuto ka talaga, wala kang dapat isipin.

**[03:30:05.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004556)  
Even sa basketball, maglalaro ka, even sa bowling, mag-target mo, alam ni Anti-Commissioner Bongko yan, na focus talaga itong sports parati.

**[03:30:16.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004557)  
So ibig sabihin, dapat po ay supportado ang ating mga athletes sa kanilang mga pangailangan, equipments, pagkain, allowances,

**[03:30:24.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004558)  
and of course, moral support ibigay natin sa kanila.

**[03:30:29.760] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004559)  
Sabi ko, malaking karangalan po ang daladala nila para sa ating parati tuwing nananalo sila.

**[03:30:35.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004560)  
Hindi po matutumbasan and we should be proud of it sa kanilang pagko-compete parati at daladala nila yung ating bandila.

**[03:30:44.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004561)  
So salamat po sa inyong lahat. I'm so happy na nandito po kayo.

**[03:30:48.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004562)  
I would also like to reiterate my full support for this agency.

**[03:30:54.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004563)  
I would also like to thank the agencies and their respective mandates.

**[03:30:56.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004564)  
Ito pong Gaban PSC. Both agencies play important and complementary roles in strengthening Philippine sports.

**[03:31:03.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004565)  
Magkaiba man po ang kanilang mandato, isa pong layunin ang mapalakas ang Philippine sports at mapangalagaan ang kapakanan po ng bawat atletang Pilipino.

**[03:31:12.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004566)  
We have seen the remarkable achievements of our Filipino athletes in various international competitions,

**[03:31:18.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004567)  
including the recently concluded 2026 Asian Games po sa Nagoya.

**[03:31:24.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004568)  
Ako mismo ay pumunta po doon.

**[03:31:26.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004569)  
Hindi po sapat na purihin lang natin sila kapag nananalo at nag-uwi ng medalya.

**[03:31:31.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004570)  
We must ensure that they receive adequate and sustained support from grassroots development to international competitions and even throughout their professional careers.

**[03:31:42.940] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004571)  
We are prepared to support additional funding for programs that will directly benefit our athletes and strengthen sports development nationwide.

**[03:31:52.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004572)  
Patuloy po natin ipaglalaman.

**[03:31:54.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004573)  
Ang kapakanan ng ating mga atleta.

**[03:31:57.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004574)  
Again, I just ask the PSC and Gab to utilize your budgets prudently.

**[03:32:03.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004575)  
Gamitin lang po ito sa tama at huwag itong masayang.

**[03:32:07.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004576)  
Make sure na mapunta talaga ito sa ating mga atleta at sa pagpapalakas ng sports dito sa ating bansa.

**[03:32:13.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004577)  
With that, the budgets of the Philippine Sports Commission and the Games and Amusement Board are hereby submitted to plenary,

**[03:32:21.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004578)  
taking into consideration the discussions.

**[03:32:24.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004579)  
Discussions today.

**[03:32:25.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004580)  
This hearing is hereby suspended.

**[03:32:28.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004581)  
Salamat po and congratulations sa inyo.

**[03:32:30.740] GAB Chairperson Atty. Francisco J. Rivera** (asr-004582)  
Thank you, Chairman Bong.
